## 69. शुभ योग — the additive muhurat vocabulary (PRD-27)

**Purpose.** Name what is specially *right* about a day, in exactly the register the dosha vocabulary already uses: a shubh yoga is **present or absent, with its window stated** — never a score, a percentage, a "luckiest day", or an offset against a dosha. The engine had twelve `DoshaKey`s and zero yogas; this section is the other half of that axis, and deliberately nothing more (round 2 §3.3 rejected the standalone day-quality score; round 1 §3 rejected standalone dosha warnings — both stances hold here).

**The v1 set.** सर्वार्थ सिद्धि (vāra × nakshatra), अमृत सिद्धि (the stronger one-per-vāra pair — always also a सर्वार्थ row), रवि योग (inclusive Sun→Moon nakshatra count ∈ 4/6/9/10/13/20), and द्विपुष्कर / त्रिपुष्कर (भद्रा tithi × रवि/मंगल/शनि vāra × the rashi-spanning nakshatra sets). Guru/Ravi Pushya stay where they ship — the computed abujh days (§60) — one concept, one home; a Sunday-Pushya day may honestly carry both the abujh card and a सर्वार्थ सिद्धि chip.

**Engine (`panchang/shubhYoga.ts`).** Pure (kundali-style boundary; source-purity test): `computeShubhYogas(p, nextSunrise)` cuts the vedic vāra-day [sunrise, next sunrise) at every solved anga end (kshaya ends included — the shipped kshaya-aware `angaAt`, never `index + 1` across a kshaya), evaluates each segment at its start (रवि's Sun nakshatra too — recorded v1 variant), and merges adjacent matches into windows; a window never crosses the next sunrise, where the vāra factor changes. Tables are pinned **row-for-row** against `docs/roadmap/conventions/shubh-yoga-v1.md` and ship DRAFT (`SHUBH_YOGA_SOURCE.verified: false`, test-pinned, release-gating — RULEBOOK §24). React reaches it through `useShubhYoga` (a thin composition over `useMuhurat` — the shared day store, no private cache) and, on the finder, `shubhYogasForDate` (`muhuratFinderScan.ts`, the `verdictForDate` two-solve contract). Nothing in `DayInputs` changes, so **no `PANCHANG_DAY_CACHE_VERSION` bump** belongs to this feature.

**The naming collision (hard rule, RULEBOOK §24).** The day card's existing योग field is one of the 27 **nitya** yogas — an unrelated Sun+Moon calculation, one of which is literally named सिद्धि. So: that field is labelled **नित्य योग / Nitya Yoga** everywhere it renders (anga tile §33.4, Daily Muhurat card §33); a shubh yoga always renders under a **शुभ योग** group label with its full "… योग" name; neither may ever render in the other's slot.

**Surfaces.** ① Panchang day card — `ShubhYogaCard` under the anga grid (§33.4): zero chrome on absent days. ② Daily Muhurat (`MuhuratCardBody`, full variant) — a शुभ योग row per present yoga after नित्य योग/करण (§33); the share variant deliberately omits it, like those rows. ③ Event Muhurat Finder (§60) — chips on result cards, the shared card on the day detail (excluded days included — doshas and yogas coexist and are never netted), dosha chips on the excluded answer block through the same component. **`MuhuratChip`** is the one chip: `tone: 'yoga' | 'dosha'`, word + tint (§12), no filled/"strong" variant — a hierarchy between yogas would be a score wearing a costume.

**Time formatting (hard rule).** These windows run nakshatra-to-nakshatra and routinely end past midnight. Every end renders through the shipped `formatEndInstant` (via `formatRangeEndAware` where a range is shown) — 24-hour clock + short-date suffix on a different civil day. The printed-panchang extended-hour style (26:12) exists nowhere in the app and must not be introduced. End-only rendering is allowed solely where the window opens at sunrise (the anga-row convention on the Daily Muhurat card); a mid-day onset always shows its start.

**Files.** `panchang/shubhYoga.ts` · `panchang/useShubhYoga.ts` · `shubhYogasForDate` in `panchang/muhuratFinderScan.ts` · `formatRangeEndAware` in `panchang/muhuratFormat.ts` · `components/{MuhuratChip,ShubhYogaCard}.tsx` · surfaces in `screens/PanchangScreen.tsx`, `components/MuhuratCardBody.tsx`, `screens/{MuhuratResultsScreen,MuhuratDayDetailScreen}.tsx`. Tests: `panchang/__tests__/shubhYoga.test.ts` (tables vs doc, mechanics, 2026 sweep, purity, the eventMuhurat-must-not-import guard), `components/__tests__/ShubhYogaCard.test.tsx` (tones, absent-is-nothing, the past-midnight suffix); device path `.maestro/shubh-yoga-smoke.yaml` (the stable relabels + journeys; a specific chip is date-dependent, pinned in unit tests instead). See RULEBOOK §24 and `docs/roadmap/prds/27-shubh-yoga.md`.

## 70. कुल परम्परा — Janma Tithi & the Family Record (PRD-29)

**Purpose.** The record-keeping inverse of §63: the app kept the tithis of the dead with real care and not one tithi of the living. Part A surfaces every saved birth profile's **जन्म तिथि** — the Hindu birthday is a tithi, not a Gregorian date — with this year's solved date, one opt-in reminder, and the day's traditional practice pointing only at shipped sections. Part B is the one private **कुल परम्परा** record — kuldevta/kuldevi, family temple, gotra, the family's kept observance, free-text notes — and the export that is its point. Stance, structural: every field is *chosen, never inferred* (no gotra→kuldevta mapping exists in the binary, no caste/community classification, no directory of families); the birthday stays **devotional, never social** — no greeting card, no share prompt, no age arithmetic. Nothing leaves the device except through the explicit export door.

**Engine.** Zero new solves: `janmaTithi.ts` derives the rule via `deriveTithiRuleFromDate` (the sunrise tithi of the birth civil date — udaya-vyapini, Ujjain/purnimant, the identical §63 convention, stated in words on the detail screen), dates it via `solveNextOccurrence`, and matches days via the new exported `tithiRuleMatchesDate` (the bare matcher — `entryMatchesDate` would also fire on the Pitru-Paksha mapped day, which is the dead's and wrong for the living). Answers persist through the SAME `pitruSmaranSolves` layer (tithi-keyed, person-free), but under their own `janma:` record (`reckoning: 'janma'`): since Oct 2026 a shraddha tithi lands on its aparahna day, so the two readings of one tithi can be a day apart and can no longer share a record; the warm-first/hydrate-immediately/solve-deferred reading rules of §63 apply verbatim (`useJanmaTithi.ts`).

**जन्म तिथि list (`screens/JanmaTithiListScreen.tsx`).** Two §33 ObservanceList row sections: **जीवित · जन्म तिथि** — one row per roster person (✦ lead glyph in the §63 muted circle; title = `personLabel` — name or birth date, never an invented placeholder; caption = rule in words; right column = this year's date + relative label) opening the detail; **पितृ · स्मरण तिथि** — the saved §63 entries as quiet rows into the shipped `PitruSmaranDetail`. The empty state explains that the tithi comes from saved birth details and offers ONE door to the Kundali screen — the roster is never editable here (RULEBOOK §14.5: one store, one owner). No engine or device-status footer.

**Person detail (`screens/JanmaTithiDetailScreen.tsx`).** §33 detail-hero pattern (✦ ॐ ✦ leading, `personLabel` title 24 pt, tithi caption, the saffron-tint `इस वर्ष · date · in N days` pill, published warm-first before the rows below). Information rows (§63 anatomy): जन्म (date · time · city, with the sunrise-convention note as an 11 pt italic second line), अगले वर्ष, जन्म नक्षत्र (the Moon nakshatra at the IST birth instant — the shared Lahiri primitive). Then the **स्मरण toggle** — default OFF per person, persisted in `@vedansh:janma-tithi:v1` only after the shared OS grant succeeds (a refusal leaves it honestly off; the §63 save-flow rule). Then **इस दिन की परम्परा** rows, shipped sections only: विष्णु सहस्रनाम (the dirghayu paath) plus up to two of the kuldev's own texts when the §Part-B record names a registry deity — all through `buildEntryStartTarget`, never a hand-rolled route. Birth details stay Kundali's to edit: the header's सम्पादन first makes THIS person the active one (Kundali edits whoever is active, so without it Edit — and the Remove inside it — acted on someone else), then cross-tabs to the Kundali form via `panchangTabTarget('Kundali', { editing: true })`.

**Home Today chip (`components/JanmaTithiDayChip.tsx`).** `✦ जन्म तिथि — <label>` beside the §63 chip in the Today strip's row, same muted-gold register (`goldTint` fill, `gold` border, `inkSoft` text — NEVER festive saffron), one per matching person, resolved off the render path (`useJanmaTithiForDate`, the `usePitruSmaranForDate` deferral). Tap → that person's detail via `moreTabTarget`.

**Reminder (eighth local family).** `notifications/janmaTithiReminderPure.ts` (pure planner) + `janmaTithiScheduler.ts` + headless `<JanmaTithiReminderScheduler>`: prefix `janma-tithi-reminder`, own Android channel `janma-tithi-reminders`, **one notice per person per year** — the eve at 18:00 (`कल <label> की जन्म तिथि है · <tithi>`; no day-of slot — the chip owns the day), `JANMA_TITHI_REMINDER_CAP = 8` soonest-first (dated one-shots have no user ranking; the roster caps at 8 people, so worst case = 8 pending against the shared iOS budget). Payload carries only the person id + occurrence key; tap deep-links to the detail.

**कुल परम्परा record (`screens/KulParampara{,'Edit','Export'}Screen.tsx`, store `panchang/kulParampara{,'Store','Share'}.ts`).** One record per device at `@vedansh:kul-parampara:v1` (versioned payload, ids validated against their registries on parse — a retired id degrades to the free-text half, never a crash). View: kuldev hero card (registry `DeityIcon` glyph or the family's own word), then §63-anatomy fields — कुल मन्दिर (chip `तीर्थ सूची से · <city>` when linked; row opens `TheerthDetail`), गोत्र, कुल व्रत (chip shows the next solved date via the festival engine's precomputed table; row opens `ObservanceDetail`), परिवार की बात — then the `आगे सौंपें` door. Edit: kuldevi/kuldevta pill pair, the 21-deity glyph grid (72 pt cells, selection = `saffronTint` + `saffronDeep` ring; choosing a glyph clears the free-text name and vice versa), registry search inputs for temple and vrat with free text as a first-class fallback, gotra and notes as plain inputs; the stance-guard line closes the screen. Export: a summary card listing EXACTLY what is shared; introductory device metaphors and OS/cloud/server footers are absent. The content summary names kul fields · janma tithis count · pitru tithis count, followed by one साझा करें action → versioned JSON (`format: 'vedansh-kul-parampara', version: 1`, display strings denormalized beside ids) written to cache and handed to the OS share sheet (`expo-sharing` + `expo-file-system`, the §46 share mechanics). PRD-06's backup path is still unbuilt; this envelope is designed to become its section verbatim, and import deliberately waits for PRD-06's one importer.

**Doors.** More hub साधना group, after पितृ स्मरण: **जन्म तिथि** (`more-janma-tithi`, state = NEW while the roster is empty, else `count · soonest date`) and **कुल परम्परा** (`more-kul-parampara`, state = the saved kuldev's name, else NEW) — §37 lists both.

**Files.** `mobile/src/panchang/janmaTithi.ts` · `janmaTithiPrefs.ts` · `useJanmaTithi.ts` · `kulParampara.ts` · `kulParamparaStore.ts` · `kulParamparaShare.ts` · `pitruSmaran.ts` (`tithiRuleMatchesDate`) · `components/JanmaTithiDayChip.tsx` + `JanmaTithiReminderScheduler.tsx` · `notifications/janmaTithiReminderPure.ts` + `janmaTithiScheduler.ts` + `deepLink.ts` · `screens/JanmaTithi{List,Detail}Screen.tsx` + `screens/KulParampara{,Edit,Export}Screen.tsx` · doors in `screens/MoreScreen.tsx`, chip in `components/TodayStrip.tsx` · routes in `navigation/{types,MoreStackNavigator}.tsx`. Tests: `panchang/__tests__/{janmaTithi,kulParampara}.test.ts` (tsx), `notifications/__tests__/janmaTithiReminderPure.test.ts` (tsx), `panchang/__tests__/jest/{kulParamparaStore,janmaTithiPrefs}.jest.test.ts`, `screens/__tests__/{JanmaTithiScreens,KulParamparaScreens}.test.tsx`; device path `.maestro/kul-parampara-smoke.yaml`. See `docs/roadmap/prds/29-kul-parampara.md`.

## 71. जिज्ञासा — Ask Vedansh: answer-first search, आज का विधान, answers that act (PRD-41)

**Purpose.** The one place a user types intent — the Search box — used to answer from the text corpus alone (`searchIndex.ts` indexes sections, deities, verses). जिज्ञासा makes the same box answer a *question* from the engines the app already ships: panchang, observances, muhurat, upvas, bhog, vidhi, katha, japam, vastu, sadhana. It is a **deterministic grammar, not a model** — bundled, offline, no network, no telemetry; the same question returns the same answer forever, and every answer shows its working (the §51 "no opaque verdict" rule generalised to the whole app). Below eligibility it **abstains** — a wrong tithi or parana time costs more than no answer — and falls back to the library search the user already had. Extends §50 (Browse by Purpose was the hand-built, content-only version of this) and leaves §48's Today strip and fold budget untouched.

**Engine (`mobile/src/ask/`, pure, `tsx --test`).** `fold.ts` folds Devanagari / IAST / Hinglish to one ASCII key (inherent vowel, Hindi word-final schwa deletion, spelling noise). `lexicon.ts` DERIVES every surface form from the registries — 21 deities, 130 observance rules, 13 `EVENT_RULES` occasions, the 8 dik via `DISHA_LABELS`, 7 vastu rooms, the japam mantras, 7 vidhis — plus recurring-observance **classes** (ekadashi, pradosh, purnima, amavasya, chaturthi, shivaratri, navratri) so a bare "एकादशी" means *the next one of any name*; `aliases.ts` is the only hand list. `resolve.ts` tags entities under a **specificity floor** (generic tokens — vrat, kaal, puja, din, katha — may narrow a match, never establish one), scores intents (trigger lexeme + filled slots; a missing required slot makes the intent *ineligible*, never "low confidence"; `blockers` let a broad trigger yield to its sibling), and runs the **stance guard**: predictive / personal framing ("kya mujhe naukri milegi") is *declined*, not answered. `intents/index.ts` registers 13 intents, each calling an existing engine or a **verified-only** registry accessor (a draft entry is never answerable). `engine.ts` is the single entry, loaded only through a dynamic `import()`; `launchPath.test.ts` walks the static import graph from `index.ts` and fails if any engine module is reachable at launch (§13.7 of the PRD — launch cost is zero by test, not by hope).

**Answer card (`components/AskAnswerCard.tsx`).** The §8 active-card gradient (`cardActiveFrom/To`, `cardActiveBorder`, `radii.lg`, `elevation.card`). Structure: a `goldChipBg` eyebrow **tag** (`eyebrowTextStyle` 10.5) · the **headline** in the language's title face, 19/28 · optional italic **sub** · **label · value rows** (78 pt micro label, value 14.5/22; a `tone: 'avoid'` row — the निषेध half — takes `avoid` / `avoidDeep`, the §65.1 split) · a hairline-ruled **`▸ गणना देखें / Show the working`** toggle revealing the answer's plain-language **basis** (`AskAnswer.basis`, localized, body face 11/17 muted; collapsed by default) — the raw engine-call `working` trail (`computeMuhuratDay(…)`, rule ids) is never shown to users: it renders only in `__DEV__` builds when an answer has no `basis`, and in release the toggle is hidden for such answers · the registry's own **tradition note** (12.5/19 muted) and a one-line **provenance** (11/16, never a URL) · up to **three actions**, the first a filled `saffron` pill with `onPrimary` text, the rest outlined in `cardActiveBorder`. All copy flows `contentByLang`, so gu/kn derive from the Devanagari half. `compact` tightens padding for the briefing. An optional `onShare` puts the §39 share circle right of the tag chip (`Share answer`) — Search passes it (a one-scope prose card: headline as title, sub, each `label — value` line, note); the card itself stays provider-free, and the compact briefing cards omit it.

**Abstain card (`AskAbstainCard`).** Deliberately as designed as the answer: `parchment-soft`, dashed `cardActiveBorder`. Two registers — an ordinary miss (*इसका उत्तर अभी नहीं दे सकते।* + "here's what the library found" / "the library found nothing either") with **did-you-mean chips** (`saffronTint` pills naming an intent's example question, tap re-asks), and the **stance-guard decline** (*यह प्रश्न भविष्य या व्यक्तिगत निर्णय का है — वेदांश़ इसका उत्तर नहीं देता।* + a redirect to what it does answer) with no chips. The ordinary-miss card appears only when the query is **question-shaped** (`looksLikeQuestion`: interrogatives, a trailing "?", or a domain word like मुहूर्त / भोग / दिशा) — a plain library query renders exactly as 1.4.6. The **decline** renders whenever the engine takes that stance, even past the question-shape gate: `DECLINE_LEXEMES` only match predictive/personal framings ("will i get the job" carries no interrogative lexeme), so a plain library query still can never see it.

**Search box (`screens/SearchScreen.tsx`).** Same field, same position. `useAsk()` loads the engine on mount (one dynamic import, warmed) so the first keystroke resolves; the resolution is memoised per trimmed query and re-runs live per keystroke (~0.3 ms V8 / ~1.3 ms Hermes-derated per resolve on a 2,900-form lexicon; no debounce needed). The answer or abstain card is the results list's `ListHeaderComponent`; when an ask header owns the space the zero state is not drawn. The placeholder **rotates through real answerable questions** every 3.2 s while idle — the feature's own discovery surface — and stops the moment the user types. An action tap commits the query to Recent, dismisses the keyboard and routes through `ask/actions.ts` (Home targets push in place; Panchang/More targets carry `initial: false` via the §60 `panchangTabTarget` / `moreTabTarget` helpers). Route params: `Search: { seed?, initialQuery? }` — an arriving `seed` (ask-from-context, §71 Phase 3) clears any previous query, since the hand-off `navigate()`s to the existing Search instance and the stale text would otherwise prefix the follow-up.

**आज का विधान (`screens/TodayVidhanScreen.tsx`, Home stack `TodayVidhan`).** The Phase 2 briefing is **standing questions asked on the user's behalf through the same resolver** (`ask/briefing.ts`): *आज* (panchang.day) · *आज का व्रत / पर्व* — the observance being kept, seeded with today's rule — or *आगे आ रहा है* — the next within 7 days · *शुभ-अशुभ समय* (muhurat.now) · *आपका संकल्प* (sadhana.progress, only when one is running). Each section is an eyebrow (11, tracked) over a `compact` AskAnswerCard. `ReaderHeader variant="index"`, an italic lede, and a closing *कुछ और पूछें* row into Search. The briefing module is a dynamic import composed after `InteractionManager` — pushing the screen never blocks a frame, and its panchang solves come from the shared day store the Today strip has usually warmed. Discovery: a Home DISCOVER `FeatureCard` (`jijnasa`, "?" glyph, NEW) — the §32 awareness-door convention — rather than a change to the Today strip, whose fold budget §50 records as contested.

**Answers that act / ask-from-context.** Every answer's actions are typed `AskTarget`s (ObservanceDetail, MuhuratDetail, MuhuratResults prefilled with the occasion, VidhiDetail with the next date, VratKathaReader, JapamCounter preset to the mantra, VastuDisha, PanchangHome on the day, SadhanaProgramDetail). `ObservanceDetailScreen` carries an *इस व्रत के बारे में पूछें* row beneath the Follow / Katha actions that opens Search **seeded** with that rule, so "iska bhog kya hai" / "kaise kare" resolve without the user naming it again.

**Stance guards (pinned).** No LLM, no network, no conversation; no prediction or personal advice (`declined`); no draft content; no luck score, dosha alarm or remedy upsell (§51 carries over); the library search never regresses (§3.6 of the PRD). Voice input (Phase 4) is a native STT dependency and therefore a store release — not shipped here.

**Deferred with reasons.** The briefing's widget variant (native extension bridge → store release) and a notification family (needs a decision on the shared iOS pending budget, §38).

**Files.** `mobile/src/ask/{types,fold,aliases,lexicon,resolve,engine,useAsk,actions,briefing}.ts` · `mobile/src/ask/intents/index.ts` · `components/AskAnswerCard.tsx` · `screens/{SearchScreen,TodayVidhanScreen,ObservanceDetailScreen,HomeScreen}.tsx` · `navigation/{types,HomeStackNavigator}.tsx`. Tests: `src/ask/__tests__/{fold,lexicon,resolve,corpus,briefing,launchPath}.test.ts` (tsx, `npm run test:ask`; the golden corpus gates ≥ 85% top-1 and zero wrong answers — 199/199 at ship), `components/__tests__/AskAnswerCard.test.tsx` (Jest); device paths `.maestro/ask-answer-smoke.yaml`, `ask-abstain-smoke.yaml`, `ask-briefing-smoke.yaml`. See RULEBOOK §25, `docs/roadmap/prds/41-jijnasa-ask-vedansh.md`, and the clickable prototype `docs/jijnasa-ask-prototype.html`.


---

## 73. दान-पुण्य — Educate-First Giving: Journey, Ledger & Daan-Dwaar (PRD-26)

**Purpose.** The third limb of the japa–vrat–daan tripod: *educate first* (why the tradition gives, in the shastra's own words), a private on-device ledger second, and an external hand-off to verified organizations strictly last. The IA contract (PRD-26 §2.7, **relaxed**) makes *educate* the DEFAULT, not a hard gate: the home carries **two standing doors in a sticky action bar** — a filled `दान करें` button and an outlined `दान-द्वार` button, both present every day and one tap away at every scroll position — so giving is never buried without the home ever becoming a pitch (the content above the bar is what opens first; the bar is a floor, not a wall). The September 2026 UX review replaced the earlier centred door block (a small floating pill over a 13pt text link with a ~26pt tap height, closed by an 11.5pt gold line at ~3.1:1) with this bar, and made the verse spine and the kathas horizontal so the home is one scroll, not three. The directory is still never a tab; the journey's terminal actions are not step-gated but sit at the end of one skippable scroll, with a quiet skip affordance near the top for the already-informed, and "record in my register" still leads "give elsewhere". **The app still never transacts** (Resolution A, links-only): every hand-off is `Linking.openURL` to the organization's own site behind the honest interstitial, and the ledger is never gated. No punya scores, streaks, totals, goals or fear copy anywhere — pinned by the copy-guard test.

**दान-पुण्य home (`screens/DaanPunyaScreen.tsx`).** Sits on a `parchment` root with the faded **Annapurna plate** (`assets/backgrounds/daan-annapurna-bhiksha.webp` via `getDaanBackground()` — Annapurna ladling anna into Shiva-as-bhikshu's patra, Kashi spires behind) behind a transparent SafeAreaView — a **1024×1536 portrait** plate, `cover`-fitted full screen under the standard overlay; both figures sit inside the central ~60% so the phone's side crop never reaches them, and the ghat steps in the lower third are drawn light enough to carry the cards. ReaderHeader (index) whose **right slot is the खाता door** (`daan-ledger-door`, a 44pt `parchmentSoft`/`divider` circle carrying a 20pt `saffronDeep` stroke ledger glyph in `react-native-svg`, a11y label `Daan ledger`) → `DaanLedger`; ungated, in reach from the first frame. Under it the 13pt `inkSoft` subtitle, then the sections in §4a order:
- `आज के दान का महत्व` — the first daan-covered observance of today (`useObservancesForDate` → `getDaanOccasionForRule`; card absent on uncovered days, never a placeholder) with the journey door (`daan-today-journey`).
- `इस वार का दान` — a label row whose right end names today in `saffronDeep` (`आज · सोमवार`), then **7 equal-width 36pt chips** (`daan-vaar-*`, muhurat chip idiom) carrying the **readable short name** (`रवि · सोम · मंगल · बुध · गुरु · शुक्र · शनि`, derived by stripping `वार`; `Sun · Mon …` in en — never the old two-letter codes), each over a 5pt dot that is `saffron` under today's chip and transparent elsewhere (the label row names the day, so colour is never the only carrier). The `goldChipBg` line (`daan-vaar-line`) sets `<वार> — <ग्रह>:` in the title face on `ink` and the items in the body face.
- `शास्त्र क्या कहते हैं` — a label row with a quiet `स्वाइप करें →` hint, then the verse spine as a **horizontal carousel** (`daan-principle-carousel`: paged `ScrollView`, card width = screen − 2×`readingGutter` − 28pt peek, 10pt gap, `snapToInterval`, `decelerationRate="fast"`). Each `daan-principle-*` card (`parchmentSoft`, `divider`, `radii.lg`, `elevation.card`) shows the verse in full (14.5/24 title face, centred; Devanagari via `verseLinesByLang`), the cite at 10.5 uppercase in **`inkMuted`** (was `gold` — 3.1:1 at that size), and the meaning **in full** (13/20 body face, `inkSoft`; no clamp and no `पूरा अर्थ` toggle — every meaning fits its card, so the toggle only hid text the card already had room for); the Gita card alone also carries the `गीता में पढ़ें ›` chip (`daan-gita-link`) deep-linking `GitaReader {chapter:17}`. Every card ends with an action row carrying the §39 share circle (`Share verse`, trailing; the Gita card's chip leads the same row) → the verse card (shloka or, for a teaching row, its title; cite as label; meaning); the journey's शास्त्र card carries the same circle top-right, with its verse lines padded 36 each side to clear it. Position dots under the carousel (active 16×6 `saffron`, rest 6×6 `dotRest`; hidden from a11y).
- `दान की कथाएँ` — a **horizontal shelf** (`daan-katha-shelf`) of 156pt tiles (`daan-katha-*`, `parchmentSoft`, `divider`, `radii.md`, `elevation.card`, min-height 96): a `gold` `॥` glyph, the title 14.5/**22** and subtitle 11.5/17 `inkMuted` (the §2 ≥1.5× floor — at 20 the semibold Devanagari matras of दानवीर/शिबि were sliced), each → `DaanKatha`.
- **Nothing after the shelf.** The scroll ends on the kathas: the stance line went in the Sept 2026 copy pass (§1), and a closing line about where the register is stored is banned outright by the customer-copy guard (`userFacingImplementationCopy.test.ts` — no `on this device` / `इस उपकरण पर` copy anywhere in `screens/` or `components/`). The ledger's privacy is a property of the feature (AsyncStorage, no network, no totals), not a caption.

The **sticky action bar** (`daan-home-actions`) is absolutely positioned at the bottom of the SafeAreaView, above the tab bar: 74pt, `parchmentHighlight`, 1pt `divider` top rule, `elevation.lifted`, `readingGutter` sides, two 50pt pills 10pt apart — the OUTLINED `दान-द्वार ›` (`daan-home-dwaar`, flex 1, 1.5pt `saffron` border, 15pt `saffronDeep`) → `DaanDirectory {}`, then the FILLED `दान करें` (`daan-home-donate`, flex 1.3, `saffron`, 16pt `onPrimary`) → `DaanJourney` — carrying `{occasionId}` when today's observance carries a daan occasion, else `{}` for the daily variant. The scroll content pads its bottom by the bar height so nothing hides under it.

**दान-यात्रा (`screens/DaanJourneyScreen.tsx`).** The §4a journey as ONE skippable scroll (`ScrollView`, root `daan-journey-screen`) — महत्व → शास्त्र → कथा (present only when the occasion carries one) → क्या दें → संकल्प-भाव — each under a gold uppercase section label; the stepper (dots, पीछे/आगे pills, `daan-journey-next`/`-prev`) is **gone**. Near the top, under the header, a quiet text skip button (`daan-journey-skip`, copy `पहले से जानता हूँ — सीधे दान-द्वार ›` / `Skip to the daan-dwaar ›`) scrolls straight to the terminal block via a `ScrollView` ref + the terminal's `onLayout`-captured Y. **Terminal actions are ALWAYS present** at the end of the sankalp section (§2.7 relaxed — no step gating): `daan-journey-record` (filled saffron, leading), `daan-journey-directory` (outlined, trailing, copy `दान-द्वार (बाहरी)`), preceded by the **recommended-daan chip row** (`daan-journey-causes`, Sept 2026): a 12.5pt `saffronDeep` body-face lead (`इस दिन का दान —` in occasion mode, `आज का दान —` in daily mode) followed by one 36pt pill chip per cause (`daan-journey-recommended-<cause>`, `goldChipBg` fill on a 1pt `gold` border, `radii.pill`, 13pt `saffronDeep` title face, `<cause name> ›`), wrapping. Each chip → `DaanDirectory {causes: [thatOne]}` — a single cause, so the द्वार skips its grid and opens that shelf directly (Wednesday's gau-gras → गौ-सेवा). Chips derive from the day's causes ∩ causes a verified org serves (a chip never lands on an empty grid); the row is absent when none survive. The outlined door beside it is the **full listing** — the recommendation and the listing are two different doors. **Nothing follows the buttons** — the "रुक जाना भी पूर्ण है" line that used to close the block is gone (copy rule below); the `(बाहरी)` in the directory button's own label already says the door leaves the app. **Two modes.** *Occasion mode* (`route.params?.occasionId` resolves via `getDaanOccasion`): title/why/items/causes/katha from the occasion; record → `DaanEntry {occasionId}`, directory → `DaanDirectory {causes}`. *Daily mode* (param absent/unresolved): built from today's vaar (`DAAN_VAAR_ENTRIES[new Date().getDay()]`) — title `दान-यात्रा`, a vaar-derived mahatva, the `sattvik-daan` (fallback `shraddhaya-deyam`) principle for shastra, the vaar's `itemsHi/itemsEn` for क्या दें, and the vaar row's `causes` for the recommended chips (Sun/Mon अन्न · Tue/Fri/Sat अन्न + वस्त्र · Wed गौ · Thu विद्या + अन्न); no katha; record → `DaanEntry {}`, directory → `DaanDirectory {}` (the full listing — never pre-filtered in daily mode). Shipped kathas open `VratKathaReader` cross-tab; teaching-kathas open `DaanKatha`.

**खाता (`screens/DaanLedgerScreen.tsx` + `DaanEntryScreen.tsx`, `contexts/DaanLedgerContext.tsx`).** A smaran register: entries grouped by civil month, each row category + panchang tithi stamp (`makeTithiStamp` — lunar month, paksha, tithi, vara, hi/en) + optional note; गुप्त entries render date + `गुप्त दान` on a `goldChipBg` card — their note/amount/occasion are stripped BEFORE persistence (`sanitizeLedgerEntry`; validator rejects unsanitized gupt rows). Storage `@vedansh/daan-ledger:v1` (versioned payload, tolerant hydration — the PRD-17 pattern). No totals anywhere; the CSV export (`buildLedgerCsv`, gupt rows stay bare even there) leaves via the OS `Share` sheet only. The entry form: auto tithi field, 9 category chips (`daan-cat-*`; only date+category required — the 10-second U6 form), the गुप्त switch (removes the detail fields from the tree), note + amount via `TextField variant="form"`. The ledger's caption is the privacy fact alone (`निजी — केवल इस डिवाइस पर`); the **empty state states the state and the next step** (`अभी कोई दान दर्ज नहीं है। ऊपर से पहला दान दर्ज करें, या सुदामा की कथा पढ़ें।`) over the `daan-ledger-empty-katha` chip — it names Sudama as a door, not as a parable told to an empty screen. The entry form's footer names what else may be recorded (`गेट पर, हुंडी में, पड़ोस में दिया दान भी यहाँ दर्ज किया जा सकता है।`) and stops there.

**दान-द्वार, by प्रयोजन (`screens/DaanDirectoryScreen.tsx` + `DaanDirectoryDetailScreen.tsx`).** The द्वार is organised on the **cause axis** (`data/daan/causes.ts` — अन्न-जल · गौ · बाल · वृद्ध · विद्या · आरोग्य · वस्त्र · जीव · आपदा), NOT the dravya axis the ledger records. The screen **opens on a cause grid, then drills into one cause** — under the `देशे काले च पात्रे` line and the subhead that states the screen's order (*first why each cause matters, then the places doing that seva, and the giving on their own website or donation page*), the default state is a grid (`daan-directory-grid`) of cause tiles (`daan-cause-tile-*`, `parchment-soft`, `radii.lg`, `elevation.card`), each showing the cause name + its "whom this serves" line and carrying **no place counts**. Occasion-supplied causes ring gold (`goldChipBg` + `cardActiveBorder`). **Tiles are derived from the rows present**, so a cause with no verified row renders no tile and no shelf. There is no give/open affordance on this screen.

**Tapping a tile drills into the single filtered cause** (`daan-directory-filtered`): an active chip `daan-cause-${id}` (muhurat chip idiom; tapping it clears back to the grid), then the `CauseHeading` — the uppercase cause label, then the **mahatva card** (`daan-cause-mahatva-*`, `parchment-soft` on `divider`, body 13/21 `ink-soft`) carrying `mahatva{Hi,En}` — why this daan is held dear — with a gold citation line beneath it where the mahatva makes a textual claim (अन्न, गौ, बाल, विद्या, वस्त्र carry one; वृद्ध, आरोग्य, जीव, आपदा are plain tradition-register prose and carry none) — and only then the org rows (`daan-org-*`). Each row shows name → its ONE about line → a gold `kind` meta line (अन्नक्षेत्र · देवस्थान ट्रस्ट · सेवा-संस्था · सेवा-पोर्टल, plus `· धन नहीं` where the hand-off is non-monetary). The teaching is the larger half of the drilled view by construction; the places are thin rows underneath.

**A single-cause arrival opens filtered directly.** When `route.params.causes` from an occasion resolves to one cause the screen skips the grid and lands on that cause's filtered view, headed by a `saffron-deep` `इस दिन की सेवा — …` line naming the day's causes (`daan-directory-occasion-line`); the active chip still clears back to the grid. The footer states plainly that the app does not represent these places.

**Detail is a place, not a profile.** Centred name → the single `daan-org-about` line → an OPTIONAL `इनके बारे में` / "About this work" section (`daan-org-description`, 13/22 `ink-soft`, rendered only when the row carries `descriptionHi`/`descriptionEn` — a 3–4 line factual read of the seva, never in the list row, never a pitch, no PII, no scale figures) → the non-monetary card where it applies → related teaching-katha → the action row (record filled + leading; `आधिकारिक वेबसाइट` outlined) over the "ऐप इस लेन-देन का हिस्सा नहीं है — दान संस्था की अपनी आधिकारिक वेबसाइट पर होगा" line. **A `directPay` row** (its `officialUrl` is the org's own hosted donation page, not its website, per RULEBOOK §27.8) swaps that copy to `दान-पृष्ठ` / "Donation page" on the button and "…दान संस्था के अपने दान-पृष्ठ पर होगा" / "…on the organization's own donation page" in the line and interstitial title, so a payment page is never called a website. There is **no registration/80G text and no verification badge**: verification stays an editorial discipline in `directory.ts` (`verifiedOn` drives the 18-month staleness drop and is never rendered). The outlined button opens the inline interstitial (`daan-org-interstitial`, naming the host) before `Linking.openURL(officialUrl)` — one link per row, the organization's own official page or hosted donation page; after a hand-off ONE gentle record-offer card renders (`daan-org-return-offer`) — declining is silent.

**दान-कथा (`screens/DaanKathaScreen.tsx`).** The header carries the §39 share circle **on the right** (`Share katha`), leaving the read-aloud pill in the language row — the katha-reader placement (§39) → a one-scope prose share of the story, then `शिक्षा` + the teaching + the source line (§39.4). Carries the shared reader furnishings under the `ReaderHeader` so the story reads like every other katha reader (Sept 2026): a centred `LanguageToggle` row with the `ReadAloudButton` pinned right (`daan-katha-{sourceId}` session over the story prose, via `useReaderReadAloud` — the section's `paragraphs*` mapped to the adapter's prose `body*` shape), then the subtitle, the shared `Ornament` divider, and the story paragraphs at the shared `meaning` reading token (`meaningToken`, `ink` in English else `ink-soft`) via `commentaryByLang` — no longer the bespoke 14/25. Then the `शिक्षा` panel (`daan-katha-teaching`: `goldTint` on a 1pt **`gold`** border, `radii.lg`, `elevation.card`; a 12.5pt `saffronDeep` title-face label — the Devanagari label is never tracked — over the teaching at **16/26** `saffronDeep` title face), the rendered canonical source line (12/18 `inkMuted`), the ॥ ॐ ॥ endcap. **The story never dead-ends.** Under a `divider` rule comes the onward block: the `अगली कथा` label row with **five position dots** (active 14×6 `saffron`, rest 6×6 `dotRest`; a11y-hidden) over a 62pt row card (`daan-katha-next`: `gold` `॥` in a 40pt `goldTint` circle, the next teaching-katha's title 15.5/22 and subtitle 12/17, a `saffronDeep` chevron) that `navigation.replace`s to the next katha in registry order, wrapping from the last to the first (rendered only when more than one katha is verified); then the `इस भाव से` label over the FILLED 52pt `दान करें` pill (`daan-katha-donate`, `saffron`, 16pt `onPrimary`) → `DaanJourney {}` (the daily variant — a katha carries no occasion), and the QUIET `खाते में दर्ज करें` text door (`daan-katha-record`, 13.5 `saffronDeep`, 36pt min-height) → `DaanEntry {}`. Nothing follows the doors — see the copy rule below. No give/pay control — §2.7 holds here as on every daan surface.

**Doors.** More hub → साधना group row `दान-पुण्य / Daan Punya` (`more-daan-punya`, NEW state for one release). **Home tab presence (PRD-26 v3):** a non-content launcher card `दान-पुण्य` (icon key `daan`, `shortNameEn: 'Giving'`, NEW badge, §18) closes the category grid full-width → `DaanPunya`; the duplicate full-width नित्य साधना category card was removed because the dedicated RoutineBanner above the grid already owns that Home door. A **DISCOVER spotlight** card (`key: 'daan-punya'`, `FeatureIcon name="daan-punya"`, NEW) → `DaanPunya`; and a **FOR TODAY** strip card (`for-today-daan`, §50) that appears on daan-covered observance days → `DaanJourney {occasionId}`. Observance Detail → `इस दिन का दान` — **always the last section** (`observance-daan-door`), rendered only when `getDaanOccasionForRule(ruleId)` matches (exact ids beat suffix families; uncovered days render nothing). **Purpose bridge:** `PurposeList` (the text-intent screen) renders a quiet `दा` educate door (`purpose-daan-door`) → `DaanPunya`, and ONLY for the two intents the app's own vocabulary shares with a seva — विद्या→`knowledge`, आरोग्य→`health` (`causeForPurpose`). `wealth`/`prosperity` are deliberately unbridged: pointing "give here" at a prosperity intent is the fruit-promise §2.2 bans; a source-shape test pins that this door opens the educate home and never the द्वार. All daan screens are registered on the More, Panchang AND Home stacks (PRD-19 multi-stack pattern) so every door pushes in place.

**Copy rule — the §1 house rule, and where it started.** The feature shipped with a muted stance line closing almost every screen — *"यहाँ रुक जाना भी पूर्ण है"*, *"मात्रा नहीं, भाव मापदण्ड है। यहाँ कोई अंक, कोई लक्ष्य, कोई तुलना नहीं"*, *"यह सूची जान-बूझकर छोटी रखी गई है"*, *"यही प्राथमिक मार्ग है"*, a screen narrating its own section order. They restated the product's stance to a reader who never asked, and read as filler under the very buttons they sat beneath, so they are **gone from every daan surface**. What stays is a line that carries a **fact the user acts on**: what else may be recorded in the register, that the giving happens on the organization's own website (or its own donation page, for a `directPay` row), and that the app neither transacts nor represents these places (§6.1 — kept once per screen, never twice). The stance itself is enforced where it belongs: in the data (no totals, the gupt guarantee, the copy-guard test's banned vocabulary), not in a caption. The teaching register stays fully intact in the **content** — the verse meanings, the cause mahatva, the kathas' शिक्षा — which is what the reader came for.

**Release.** Pure JS + AsyncStorage — OTA-safe. The store-policy review gate (PRD-26 §6.1) still applies to the release *carrying the directory*: it rides a store release for review visibility even without a native module.

**Files.** `mobile/src/data/daan/{types,principles,occasions,vaar,kathas,directory,ledger,index}.ts` · `contexts/DaanLedgerContext.tsx` · `screens/Daan{Punya,Journey,Ledger,Entry,Directory,DirectoryDetail,Katha}Screen.tsx` · doors in `screens/MoreScreen.tsx` + `screens/ObservanceDetailScreen.tsx` · registrations in `navigation/{MoreStackNavigator,PanchangStackNavigator}.tsx` + `navigation/types.ts` (DaanStackParamList) · provider in `App.tsx`. Tests: `src/data/daan/__tests__/daanContent.test.ts`, `src/contexts/__tests__/DaanLedgerContext.test.tsx`, `src/screens/__tests__/DaanScreens.test.tsx`; device path `.maestro/daan-punya-smoke.yaml`. See RULEBOOK §27 and `docs/roadmap/prds/26-daan-punya.md`.

---

## 72. प्रश्न — current phase, direction and expandable Jyotish evidence (PRD-43)

**Purpose.** Explain what the person's natal graha, running Mahadasha/Antardasha and today's gochar mean together for their question. The phase decision now covers adult career, business, study, money, marriage and travel. `buildPrashnaReading` returns the full `analysis`, legacy `guidance`, and an optional serializable `phase`; pilot consumers use `phase`. Health, mind, children and minors retain safety-limited themes and actions without a timed verdict. All app computation remains offline and deterministic. The current comparison lives in `docs/evaluations/prashna-phase-2026-09-21/`; its source register is `docs/roadmap/conventions/prashna-phase-v1.md`.

**Customer copy.** Explain the phase, dates, Jyotish reasons and direction. Ask’s expandable working contains localized basis chains, without function names, version IDs, scoring traces or review-status prose. An age-gated answer needs no technical working trace. Meaningful sharing-content notices and deletion consequences remain.

**Entry and context.** Keep the full-width Prashna lead card on the saved Jyotish landing and the existing Ask entry. The screen identifies the active person, age and as-of date. Nine purpose buttons preserve their age gates. Explicit question choices distinguish first job, switching, growth, starting a business and partnership; changing purpose resets to general. Missing personal circumstances are never inferred. Person/purpose/question changes reset expanded evidence (and legacy checklist state). Question rows grow with translations, have a filled radio indicator and at least 48 dp height.

**Timed adult reading order.**
1. **Direct answer** — the selected question and a qualified, topic-specific recommendation appear before the named Mahadasha/Antardasha and exact current-period dates.
2. **Why this answer** — up to two chart-linked reasons in favour and two reasons to pause, each with a compact birth-chart, running-period or current-transit reference. Missing dasha support is uncertainty, not an adverse graha. Job-switch retains its specific “In favour of a change” label.
3. **What to do now** — a question-specific real-world checkpoint. A job switch requires checking an actual offer; business needs customer feedback; study needs practice evidence; marriage needs both people's consent. Muhurat and Guna Milan hand-offs stay available for their relevant purposes.
4. **Next period change** — the next actual Antardasha boundary, including Mahadasha boundaries. This is not a promised improvement date; future gochar is not silently assumed equal to today's.
5. **See Jyotish basis** — collapsed by default, with every typed signal and BasisChain. Source-review progress and excluded methods stay in documentation and structured export metadata.
6. **Optional practice and context** — the traditional-reading limit, existing allow-listed practice and dated footer.

**Bilingual layout.** Existing parchment surfaces, saffron accents, rounded cards and tabs remain. Pilot body is Cormorant 18/25 in English and script serif 15/25 in Hindi; hero headings 22/29 and 18/29; section labels 10/20 and 12/20. Reason headings are 19/26 and 16/26. Cards have 18 dp padding and 10 dp gaps. Buttons wrap, maintain a 44 dp minimum target and use script-safe leading; the basis toggle is at least 48 dp and announces expanded state. No fixed content heights or truncated body text. Basis chips wrap within the card with localized labels. Native screenshots, including compact/large-type evidence and remaining review limits, are indexed in the evaluation README.

**Computation and provenance.** The pilot uses a question-priority natal house lord; actual lordship/occupancy links for maha/antar; and current Jupiter/Saturn placement/aspects from Lagna with Moon-relative transit conditions and vedha. Rahu/Ketu occupancy establishes topic only; no blanket favourable/difficult polarity is assigned without their missing association/dispositor analysis. The phase is a transparent decision table, not a score or probability. Contradictions survive. `origin: phase-interpretation` directions retain signal IDs. Source review stays `verified:false`; tests prove contracts, not predictive validity. Health, mind, children and minors retain basis-linked, safety-limited themes and `origin: editorial` practical suggestions.

**Freshness and integration.** `useJyotishNow` refreshes on focus, foreground, IST midnight and the next exact dasha boundary. Both Prashna and report rebuild against that instant. `gocharScanDays:0` skips future ingress scans, not the current snapshot. Ask uses the same phase engine for all six adult topics, recognizes explicit job, study and business subquestions, and otherwise keeps the general question. Full reading passes `{purposeId, questionId}` and recomputes for the active chart. The warned text export includes the selected phase, decision, detailed signals, direction provenance and next period in a separate JSON block. The final report JSON tail remains unchanged and parse-back equal.

**Files and verification.** `prashna.ts` gathers factors; `prashnaPhase.ts` combines the pilot; `prashnaGuidance.ts` composes the result and preserves legacy guidance; `PrashnaPhaseContent.tsx` renders it. Screen, Ask and handoff tests cover shared consumption. `prashnaPhase.engine.test.ts` covers factual basis, temporal change, exact period edges, vedha, contradictory signals and age/question gates. `useJyotishNow.jest.test.tsx` covers midnight, dasha and foreground refresh. `.maestro/prashna-smoke.yaml` retains legacy-study coverage and adds phase assertions for business. Evaluation-only scripts record public/synthetic cases and real external model responses; they do not introduce an app AI path.

**Status.** The adult decision format covers six topics. Protected topics and minors retain their existing safety boundaries. Fuller Jyotish interpretation, independent source sign-off and release certification remain open. The earlier generic plan was rejected in user review; it is not the accepted endpoint for those topics.

## 73. क्षेत्रीय पंचांग — the regional & sampradaya lens (PRD-42 W2)

**Purpose.** A calendar the user turns on, so a Maithil family sees Sama Chakeva where they expect it, a Jain family can reach Paryushana, and a user in Ujjain sees exactly what they see today. Two promises govern every pixel below, and both are stated **on** the surfaces rather than in a help page: **a calendar only ever adds days**, and **nothing here asks who you are**. The lens set is `@vedansh:panchang-lenses` — a registry of 22 calendars (20 regional + `jain`, `sindhi`), an empty set meaning today's app byte for byte.

**Offered ≠ registered.** Every user-facing surface below offers only the calendars that **add at least one observance in this build** — `getLensesWithContent()` (`vratCatalog.ts`), today `तमिऴ` and `जैन`. The other 20 are registered (names, examples, seeding) but never shown, counted or seeded: a switch that changes nothing was the Sept 2026 report ("I selected Jain and it still shows everything"). "n उपलब्ध" everywhere is the offered count, `useLenses().lenses` is the stored set narrowed to offered ids (a Mumbai seed from before content shipped stays on disk but is not displayed), and a calendar joins the moment its first `default` + `lens` rule lands, with no registration step. **If the offered count is ever zero, the ledger column, the quiet-day row and the More row do not render at all** — a filter that can choose nothing is not a filter.

**Structure — one sheet, four doors.** The sheet is identical from every entry; only the doorway moves.

| Surface | Where | What it says |
|---|---|---|
| **The sheet** (`LensPickerSheet.tsx`) | Bottom sheet, `parchmentHighlight`, 22 pt top radii, grabber | Title `क्षेत्रीय पंचांग`, then the "days are only ever added, never removed" line at 12.5/19 `inkSoft`, then `n सक्रिय · m और उपलब्ध` at 11 pt `inkMuted` (`सभी n सक्रिय` once every offered calendar is on). Under the count, **two pill buttons** — `सभी चुनें` and `सभी हटाएँ` — 36 pt tall, 1 pt `saffron` outline, 12.5 pt `saffronDeep` label, centred with a 10 pt gap; **each hides when it would be a no-op** (no सभी चुनें when all are on, no सभी हटाएँ with none on), so the header never offers a dead button. Rows are **only the offered calendars**, grouped उत्तर · पूर्व · पश्चिम · दक्षिण · परंपरा under 10 pt/1 pt-tracked `inkMuted` eyebrows; a group with no offered calendar is not rendered (today the sheet shows दक्षिण → तमिऴ and परंपरा → जैन). Each row: name at 15 pt `ink`, its two or three recognisable observances at 11 pt `inkMuted` (one line, ellipsised), then a **third line naming what this calendar adds** — `जोड़ता है: रोहिणी व्रत` at 11 pt `saffronDeep`, two lines max (`getRulesForLens`) — and a 22 pt circular check filled `saffron` when on. **Checkboxes, not radios** — lenses compose — and a tap applies immediately with no confirm step, because the calendar behind the sheet answers it. 52 pt row floor, ≥ 44 pt tap target. |
| **The ledger column** (`PanchangScreen.tsx`, व्रत-पर्व) | Third column of the §33 ledger bar, after मेरा व्रत and पितृ स्मरण | A `CatalogLedgerCell` inside the shared `parchmentSoft`-on-`divider` bar: ❖ glyph, `क्षेत्र` at 14 pt, and one 11 pt line of state that still **names a calendar** — `तमिऴ +1` (the first active calendar plus a count of the rest), `सभी n` when every offered calendar is on, or `n उपलब्ध` with nothing on. The column holds ~106 pt, so the names are truncated to one plus `+n` rather than turned into a bare count: a count does not tell a user where their extra dates came from, and the leading name still does. Renders even with nothing on — for a user whose city seeded nothing it is the only path to the sheet — but **not at all when the offered count is zero** (the bar falls back to two columns). |
| **आपके पंचांग से** (`PanchangScreen.tsx`, व्रत-पर्व, `LensAdditionsSection`) | Card directly under the ledger bar, **only with ≥ 1 lens on** | The additive contract leaves a lensed day unmarked among the universal ones, so this card is the pointer to what the active calendars actually added. `parchmentSoft` on `divider`, `radii.md`, `elevation.card`, 14 pt gutters. Head: `आपके पंचांग से` at 14 pt `ink` over an 11 pt `inkMuted` count line (`1 तिथि जो केवल इन पंचांगों से आती है`, or `चुने हुए पंचांग अभी कोई विशेष तिथि नहीं जोड़ते`), with a `बदलें` text button (12 pt Inter SemiBold `saffronDeep`) that opens the sheet. Then **one group per active offered calendar in registry order** under the sheet's 10 pt uppercase eyebrow: each added rule is a `resultRow` (15 pt `ink` name, 13 pt `inkMuted` other-script caption, the next date as a 11 pt `saffronDeep` uppercase short date, `›`) opening `ObservanceDetail`. (The empty-calendar line and the zero-count head copy remain in code as a guard but cannot render, since only offered calendars can be on.) Data: `getLensAdditions` + `getNextOccurrence` (`vratCatalog.ts`). |
| **The quiet-day row** (`PanchangScreen.tsx`, पंचांग) | Inside the day card, **only** where it would otherwise read `इस तिथि पर कोई व्रत या पर्व नहीं है।`, and only with ≥ 1 offered calendar | `❖ क्षेत्रीय पंचांग जोड़ें · n उपलब्ध` on `goldTint`/`divider`/`radii.md`, 13 pt title over 11 pt caption. Fixes the one structural gap of the ledger row: the tab opens on **पंचांग**, the lens changes **पंचांग**, but the row lives on the other segment. Deliberately **not dismissible** — the dismissible seed line disappears forever on one tap. Costs nothing on a day that has content, because it is not rendered then. |
| **The More row** (`MoreScreen.tsx`) | परिवार group, after जन्म तिथि; absent when the offered count is zero | `SettingsRow`, ❖ on `gold`, state = the active calendars by name (`+n` past two), `सभी n`, or `n उपलब्ध`. A second **home**, not a doorway: where a user goes looking once they half-remember the setting. |

**The seed notice.** When a chosen city seeds its calendars — and only calendars with content are ever seeded — one line appears on the पंचांग day view — where the new dates are — on `goldTint`, naming the **city's** calendar and never the person: `बेंगलुरु का कर्नाटक · तेलुगु पंचांग जोड़ा गया — बदलें`, with a ✕. Tapping the line opens the sheet. Never a modal, never a question.

**Type & motion.** Devanagari through `cardFontByLang` / `scriptBodyFont(lang, typography.meaning.fontFamily)` — never a Cormorant token, which `typographySafety.test.ts` enforces. The sheet is the standard `animationType="slide"`; a lens toggle has **no** animation of its own, because the day behind it changing is the feedback.

**A11y.** Each lens row is `accessibilityRole="checkbox"` with `checked`, labelled in English even in a Hindi UI, naming the observances and what it adds (`Jain calendar. Paryushana · Samvatsari · Gyan Panchami. Adds 1: Rohini Vrat`), so two regional calendars are distinguishable by ear. The bulk pills are buttons `Select all n calendars` / `Clear all calendars` (testIDs `lens-select-all` / `lens-clear-all`). The ledger, quiet-day and More rows are buttons labelled `Regional calendars, n on` / `, all n on` / `, none selected`. The आपके पंचांग से card is `testID="lens-additions"`; its rows are buttons `<NameEn>, next <date>`, its `बदलें` is `Change regional calendars`.

**Files.** `mobile/src/panchang/lenses.ts` (type, ids, set, parse/serialise — I/O-free and launch-path-safe), `lensRegistry.ts` (names, examples, `CITY_LENS`/`STATE_LENS` — behind a `require()` thunk), `lensStore.ts` (AsyncStorage + once-per-install seeding of offered calendars only + `setAllLenses`/`clearLenses`), `useLenses.ts` (display set via `withContentOnly`, `availableCount`, `selectAll`/`clearAll`/`allSelected`), `vratCatalog.ts` (`getLensesWithContent`, `withContentOnly`, `getRulesForLens`, `getLensAdditions`); `mobile/src/components/LensPickerSheet.tsx` (`React.lazy` in both screens); entry points and `LensAdditionsSection` in `PanchangScreen.tsx`, the row in `MoreScreen.tsx`; the gate in `festivals.ts` (`getObservanceCatalog`) and `festivalEngine.ts` (day/month/upcoming). Tests: `panchang/__tests__/lens.test.ts` (incl. the lens-by-lens agreement between `getRulesForLens` and the catalog gate), `.maestro/regional-lens-smoke.yaml` (authored; device run owed). Contract: RULEBOOK §23a.5, §23a.11–12.

---

## 74. पितृ पक्ष परिचय — the education layer beside the reminder and the vidhi (PRD-44)

**Customer copy.** Show available tithi teachings without a source-review-progress footer; review status remains in the content registry.

**Purpose.** Pitru Paksha in the app was a *reminder* (two public season notices), a *calendar* (the sixteen-row fortnight) and a *door* (the tila-tarpana guide). §74 adds the missing layer — **जानें**: what the fortnight is, why it is kept, whose day is which, what the bundled texts say, the stories, and honest answers to the common questions — and puts it **first** in the fortnight's journey (understand → see the day → act → remember) while the reminder and the vidhi stay exactly as §63 / §62 specify. It explains and never prescribes; it never names a dosha, a remedy or an obligation, and never claims the app's guide is complete shraddha (RULEBOOK §28).

**Tone.** Pitru Smaran's register (§63): Home gradient, `ReaderHeader variant="index"`, `spacing.xxl` gutters, `goldTint` / `gold` / `parchmentSoft` / ink tones; `saffron` and `saffronDeep` appear only as interactive affordance (hand-off pills, और पढ़ें, chevrons) and the quoted verse line. No NEW badge inside the feature, no streaks, no completion, no share surface, no emoji. The word is **परिचय**, never "guide" or "how-to".

**Structure — three routes in `MoreStackParamList` (`PitruPakshaShiksha: undefined` · `PitruParichayReader: {lessonId?} | undefined` · `PitruKatha: {kathaId}`), registered on `MoreStackNavigator` after `PitruPakshaOverview` so Back retraces overview → परिचय → reader / katha. Sept 2026 UX review: the परिचय screen was one scroll of six content types with three interaction models (unlock a lesson, read a verse card, tap into a story) and ~39 blocks — the content was fine, the stacking was the problem. It is now a **hub shaped by use**, and the lessons are read in the app's own reader shell:**

1. **परिचय hub** (`PitruPakshaShikshaScreen`) — Home gradient, `ReaderHeader variant="index"`, a gold `॥ ॐ ॥` (letter-spacing 6). No lede line (the section-order caption went under §1's copy rule) and no section rail (the screen no longer needs one). Each section is present only when it has verified rows; labels are the `typography.sectionLabel` uppercase idiom in `inkMuted` on the 24 pt (`spacing.xxl`) gutter. In order:
   - **Introduction — read** (`pitru-lesson-<firstId>`): one card (`parchmentSoft`, 1 px `divider`, `radii.md`) with the first lesson's title 16/24 `ink` and its **first paragraph only** at 15/25 `inkSoft`, then one filled 48 pt door (`saffron`, `radii.sm`): **पूरा परिचय पढ़ें** + `<n> पाठ ›` → `PitruParichayReader`. No lesson is unfolded in place; nothing is "and more".
   - **लोग क्या पूछते हैं — scanned** (`pitru-prashna-list`): the questions people arrive with, second, as an accordion in one ruled card. Question rows 14.5/22 title face, 48 pt, a `›` that rotates 90° when open; tapping opens the answer (13.5/22 `inkSoft`) beneath and closes any other — one answer open at a time, none at first. `accessibilityState.expanded` tracks it.
   - **शास्त्र-वचन — browsed** (`pitru-principle-carousel`): the §73 verse carousel, same geometry (card = width − 2 × gutter − 28 pt peek, 10 pt gap, `snapToInterval`, `radii.lg`, `elevation.card`) — title, quoted line(s) 14.5/24 `saffronDeep` centred (only when `ref` resolves to the verse cited), cite in the uppercase label face, meaning clamped to 2 lines with **पूरा अर्थ ›** / **संक्षेप ‹** unfolding it in place, and the reader pill **गीता में पढ़ें ›** → More-stack `GitaReader {chapter, initialIndex}` or **रामायण में पढ़ें ›** → `HomeTab/ValmikiRamayanReader`. Position dots beneath (6 pt, active 16 pt `saffron`).
   - **कथाएँ — browsed** (`pitru-katha-shelf`): the §73 tile shelf — 156 pt tiles, gold `॥`, title 14.5/22 (the ≥1.5× floor), subtitle 11.5/17 `inkMuted` → `PitruKatha`.
   - **शब्द — looked up** (`pitru-shabd-door`): a door row `<n> शब्द` + the first five terms, `›` rotating open; it unfolds the ruled glossary card beneath (term 14/21 `ink`, one line 13/20 `inkSoft`, 1 px `divider` between entries). Eleven terms are a lookup, not part of the reading flow.
   - **अब** — the three doors LAST, in the `goldTint` / `gold` door idiom (52 pt): **पक्ष की सोलह तिथियाँ** ("इस वर्ष की तारीख़ें · किस दिन किसका · आपके परिवार के दिन" — the only way from here to the fortnight, so it names what it opens) → `PitruPakshaOverview` · **॥ पितृ तिल-तर्पण स्मरण** → `VidhiDetail {vidhiId}` (undated; only when `getVidhiById('shraddha-tarpan-vidhi')` resolves) · **॥ पितृ स्मरण** → `PitruSmaranList`. No footer line.
   - **No पक्ष की तिथियाँ list.** The fortnight is the dated overview's, and its verified tithi teachings ride those rows (§63.5 item 5). The registry keeps all sixteen `tithi` rows and its coverage test.
2. **परिचय reader** (`PitruParichayReaderScreen`) — **the Vrat Katha reader shell** (§33 `VratKathaReaderScreen`), component for component, so the lessons read like every other long text in the app: flat `parchment`; `ReaderHeader` (44 pt circular back, 16 pt title, italic `n / m` counter, `sideWidth` 80); `ReadingProgressBar`; the toggle row — `LanguageToggle` centred, `ReadAloudButton` pinned right (`useReaderReadAloud`, `sourceId: 'pitru-parichay'`, the adapter's prose branch); a horizontal `pagingEnabled` `FlatList` of `KathaSectionPage`s — pill **`परिचय · n/m`** / `Introduction · n/m` (the page's `pillHi`/`pillEn` props; the katha default stays `प्रसंग`/`Part`), 20 pt title, compressed `॥` ornament, every paragraph at the shared `meaning` token (20/34 `inkSoft` hi, 17/28 `ink` en), long pages scroll inside; §5 pager dots overlaid (6 pt, current 18 × 6 `saffronDeep`); light haptic per page. Swipe only — no prev/next buttons. Pages are the **verified** `parichay` lessons in registry order; `lessonId` sets `initialScrollIndex` (an unknown or draft id falls back to page 1). The list ends on a `NextChapterCard` (`अगला · पक्ष की सोलह तिथियाँ ›`, `__type: 'transition'` so read-aloud never speaks it); settling on it fires, once, after the house 400 ms, **`navigation.popTo('PitruPakshaOverview')`** — back to the calendar if it is below in the stack (a reader opened from a dated day), a replace otherwise — so no second overview is ever stacked. The counter holds at `m / m` on the card.
3. **कथा reader** (`PitruKathaScreen`) — the §73 `DaanKathaScreen` pattern in Pitru's register, carrying the same shared reader furnishings (Sept 2026): a centred `LanguageToggle` row with the `ReadAloudButton` pinned right (`pitru-katha-{sourceId}` session over the story prose via `useReaderReadAloud`), subtitle 12.5 `inkMuted` centred, the shared `Ornament` divider, then paragraphs at the shared `meaning` token (`meaningToken`, `ink` in English else `inkSoft`) via `commentaryByLang` — no longer the bespoke 14/25 — the **शिक्षा** panel (`goldTint`, 1 px `gold`, `radii.lg`; gold uppercase label; teaching 14/23 `saffronDeep`), the rendered canon line (`स्रोत: …`, 11.5 `inkMuted`), an optional **मूल श्लोक … में पढ़ें ›** pill when the katha carries a `ref`, and the `॥ ॐ ॥` endcap at 0.7 opacity. An unresolvable (draft) id renders the header only.
4. **Overview door** — §63.5.

**Data (`mobile/src/data/pitru/`) — 61 rows authored, 39 verified.** `lessons.ts` (`kind: 'parichay' | 'tithi' | 'shabd'`, `fortnightDay` on tithi rows — 10 concept incl. the draft पञ्चबलि lesson, all 16 fortnight tithis, 13 glossary incl. the draft पञ्चबलि term), `principles.ts` (`ref: {kind:'gita'|'valmiki', chapter, verseIndex}` — Gita 1.42 / 9.16 / 9.25 / 10.29 / 2.20, Valmiki 2.102.27 / 1.41.19), `kathas.ts` (four verified, each retold from the bundled corpus and anchored by a `ref`: Ayodhya 102, Bala 38–44, Aranya 67–68, Ayodhya 77), `prashna.ts` (9); `index.ts` exposes **verified-only** accessors behind `require()` thunks (the More stack imports the overview eagerly — launch-graph rule) and the single door predicate `hasPitruShiksha()`. Scripture is deep-linked, never re-typed; review-only `source` blocks are never rendered.

**A11y.** Every pressable carries an English constant label for Maestro (flow: `.maestro/pitru-paksha-smoke.yaml`): hub — `Read the introduction`, `Question <id>` (with `expanded` state), `Expand meaning` / `Collapse meaning`, `Open gita reader for <id>` / `Open valmiki reader for <id>`, `Open katha <id>`, `Glossary` (with `expanded` state), `Open Pitru Paksha overview`, `Open Tila-Tarpana remembrance guide`, `Open Pitru Smaran list`; reader — `Back`, `Reading language`, the read-aloud pill, pages `pitru-parichay-page-<id>`, counter `pitru-parichay-counter`; overview — `Open Pitru Paksha introduction`, `Open Tila-Tarpana remembrance guide`, `Open the first family shraddha day`, `Open day <en date>` / `Close day <en date>`, `Open smaran <en name>`, `Open the lesson on how a tithi is matched`. Cards are plain information panels; only interactive rows carry chevrons.

**Privacy.** The layer reads nothing from `PitruSmaranContext`; routes carry `{kathaId}` or an undated `{vidhiId}` only; reminder payloads are unchanged.

**Files.** `data/pitru/{types,lessons,principles,kathas,prashna,index}.ts` · `screens/PitruPakshaShikshaScreen.tsx` (hub) · `screens/PitruParichayReaderScreen.tsx` (reader) · `screens/PitruKathaScreen.tsx` · `components/KathaSectionPage.tsx` (`pillHi`/`pillEn`) · the door in `screens/PitruPakshaOverviewScreen.tsx` · routes in `navigation/types.ts` + `MoreStackNavigator.tsx`. Tests: `data/pitru/__tests__/pitruShikshaContent.test.ts` (Jest — registry invariants, reader-ref resolution against the bundled JSON, stance guard), `screens/__tests__/PitruPakshaShikshaScreen.test.tsx` (hub + reader + katha), the overview cases in `PitruSmaranScreens.test.tsx`, the reader's row in `readerReadAloud.test.tsx`; e2e `.maestro/pitru-paksha-smoke.yaml` (season-gated — see its header). PRD: `docs/roadmap/prds/44-pitru-paksha-shiksha.md`; prototype: `docs/pitru-paksha-shiksha-prototype.html`. Contract: RULEBOOK §28.

## 75. Screen loading & the background warm-up

**Purpose.** Every screen but each stack's root loads on demand, so the launch stops evaluating ~1.5 MB of surfaces most sessions never open. The cost must not reappear as a wait on the tap, so screens are warmed in the background before they are needed. The user-facing contract: **a tap on a warmed screen paints its content on the first frame, with no loading state at all.** The loading state below exists only for a tap that beats the warm-up.

**Structure.**
1. **Launch.** Each stack's root (Home, More, Panchang) is eager — it *is* the first frame, and deferring it would trade launch cost for a launch spinner.
2. **Warm-up order.** After `NavigationContainer.onReady`, screens are warmed breadth-first by tap-distance from Home (depth 2, then 3, …), one module per idle slice: each waits for `InteractionManager` to go idle and yields 60 ms before the next, so an in-flight touch, navigation or animation always wins the thread. Non-screen warm-ups ride the same walk — the Panchang stack chunk and the search index, both at depth 2. The search index runs *alongside* the walk rather than inside it: it is a many-second job at background pace, so its entry starts it and returns, and the walk carries on. It is built in ~8 ms time-budgeted slices over units of one chapter or one temple, each behind `InteractionManager` plus a one-frame gap; the worst slice measured one frame (§36).
3. **Following the user.** On every navigation the current route's children (from the generated `routeGraph.ts`) move to the front of the queue, so the walk stays one tap ahead of wherever the user actually is rather than finishing a depth they have left. Ordering only — it never blocks or drops a screen.
4. **Nested stacks.** Warming a lazily-loaded stack registers its own screens; the walk drains the registry rather than snapshotting it, so those join the queue at their own depth. A route registered on several stacks (Daan, Vidhi, GitaReader, VastuDisha) has each stack's copy warmed.

**Loading state (cold tap only).** Full-screen `parchment` surface, centred `ActivityIndicator` in `saffron`. No text, no skeleton — a cold module resolves in a few milliseconds, so anything richer would flash. Announced to screen readers as a progress bar labelled **खुल रहा है · Opening** (via `contentByLang`). Cold modules resolve through a cancellable effect rather than a `use(promise)` / Suspense hide-and-reveal cycle. This avoids Android Fabric re-attaching an already-parented SVG path when a cold route opens. Warm screens still paint synchronously; once mounted, the resolved screen keeps its element type and local state across updates. A load that fails reaches the existing error boundary and is retried on the next attempt rather than cached.

**Files.** `mobile/src/navigation/lazyScreen.tsx` (the lazy wrapper, warm/cold paths, loading state) · `screenPrefetch.ts` (the walk, `prioritise`) · `dataWarmups.ts` (non-screen warm-ups) · `routeGraph.ts` (generated by `scripts/gen-route-graph.mts`) · wiring in `App.tsx` (`onReady`, `onStateChange`). Gates: `__tests__/screenPrefetch.test.tsx` (what paints, warm and cold), `__tests__/lazyScreenRegistration.test.ts` (no eager screen imports), `routeGraph.test.ts`, `src/data/__tests__/launchGraph.test.ts` (budget). Registering a new screen: RULEBOOK §2 row 8.
